## User (or harness) — 2026-11-24 22:02:08 UTC

Produce DC performance graphs (input and output ranges, linearity) for the two custom pads of the taped-out IHP sg13cmos5l Chipalooza #2 slot 7, by running simulations. These go into the designer's documentation, so they must be correct, readable, labelled, and reproducible. No invented numbers: every curve and number comes from a run you make here.

The circuits:
- S2D pad `sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff`: single-ended pad in, differential out: outp − outn = 2 (pad − vref), (outp + outn)/2 = vcm. Nominal vref ≈ 0.49 V, vcm ≈ 1.26 V (from the replica bias), iref ≈ 5 µA, supplies iovdd / vdd 3.3 V (3.0 … 3.6 V). Documented linear input range 0 … ~1 V with vref = 0.5 V (high end 1.057 … 1.299 V over corners).
- D2S pad `sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single`: differential in (vinp, vinn around vcm), pad out: pad − vref = (vinp − vinn)/2, vref (of the D2S) = vcm ≈ 1.26 V. Output swing about 0.64 … 3.28 V at 3.3 V; INL is much worse at iovdd 3.0 V (up to 87 mV in the schematic CACE).
- Default loop in the slot: pad_d2s = vcm + (pad_s2d − vref).

Sources (read-only): taped-out slot repo /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7 (branch `chipalooza2-documentation`; `pads/<cell>/<cell>.spice` and .gds; `verification/c2_gaps/d2s_pex/` has a working magic PEX netlist of the D2S pad and the method/run scripts incl. the ngspice option rshunt=1e12 needed for convergence; `verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg/` has slot-level power-up methods). Design repository https://github.com/tatzelbrumm/sg13cmos5l_cm_ip__single2diff2single (public, ~160 MB: do NOT clone whole; `git clone --depth 1 --filter=blob:none --no-checkout <url> <dir>`, `git fetch --depth 1 origin <commit>`, `git show FETCH_HEAD:<path>`, `git archive FETCH_HEAD <paths> | tar x`): commit 91a7da8b67ac56a26c605501146667a1fde28c32 (pad macros incl. CACE testbench templates tb_dc etc. and the S2D PEX view), 9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9 (slot tier-1 netlists `docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/`). Reuse the existing CACE testbench conditions where possible so the graphs agree with the committed CACE tables. Scratch: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/468ace82-a4b3-592d-8838-6ea52a1be021/scratchpad/dcgraphs.
Environment: IHP PDK /foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l (PDK_ROOT=/foss/pdks PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l), ngspice with IHP OSDI, magic, netgen, CACE 2.11, Python3 + matplotlib (install with pip --break-system-packages if missing). 4 CPUs; another job runs a light read-only task; use at most 3 parallel ngspice.

Graphs, in priority order (both schematic and post-layout PEX view of the taped-out pad where available; label which):
1. S2D DC transfer: outp, outn, differential output and output common mode vs pad input swept wide (e.g. −0.3 … 3.3 V, fine steps) at tt 27 °C 3.3 V, with the linear input range marked; then the differential output and the deviation from the ideal 2·(pad − vref) (INL-style error, mV, log or linear) over pad 0 … 1.3 V for corners tt/ss/ff/sf/fs × −40/27/125 °C (and 3.0/3.6 V supply at the worst corners). Define and state the linear-range criterion you use (e.g. error from the best-fit line < 1 mV or gain within 1 %), and give a table of the input range per corner by that criterion.
2. D2S DC transfer: pad output vs differential input (vinp − vinn swept wide, e.g. ±4 V differential around vcm or until both rails are reached), unloaded and 1 kΩ to vref/ground (state which), tt 27 °C; output range marked; then deviation from ideal vs differential input over the usable range at the same corner grid incl. iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 / 3.6 V (3.0 V is the known headroom limit). Table of output range per corner by your criterion.
3. Round trip (default loop S2D → D2S, as in the slot, with the replica vcm/vref or the nominal values — say which): pad_d2s vs pad_s2d and error vs input, tt and the corner extremes.
4. If time: input/output range vs vref and vcm settings (the S2D range_params CACE sweeps vref 0.5/0.8/1.2/1.65 and vcm 1.0…1.65) as a contour or family of curves.
Plot style: clear axis labels with units, legends, titles naming the view (schematic / PEX), corner, temperature and supply; one idea per figure; PNG at 150 dpi (< 300 kB each) and SVG if small; consistent colors per corner.

Deliverables (write ONLY here; do NOT run git commands that change state; I commit): /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/dc_curves/ with `README.md` (what each figure shows, how to read it, criteria, exact commands, tool versions incl. `ngspice -v`, source commits, what it does NOT show), `run.sh` reproducing everything from this repo + the design-repo commits (fetches what it needs), decks/scripts, `plots/`, small CSV summaries (< 300 kB each), `results.md` (range tables, key numbers). No raw files; total < 5 MB. Do not modify existing files in that repo. SPDX header in each new text file (`SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier`, `SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0`). Label everything "IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic | magic PEX)". Write partial results as each graph set finishes (I may commit mid-run).

Report in under 400 words: figure list with one line each, range tables' key numbers, failures/TBDs.

```
# D2S pad: post-layout (magic PEX) characterization

**IHP sg13cmos5l post-layout (magic PEX) simulation** of the taped-out pad `sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single` (D2S),
compared with its committed schematic CACE results. Results: [results.md](results.md).

## Why

The D2S pad had CACE results on its schematic only: the committed results (design repository commit
91a7da8b67ac56a26c605501146667a1fde28c32, `macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/verification/cace/results/`) predate
its layout, and no PEX view existed. The S2D pad has both views. This folder adds the PEX view of the D2S pad as
taped out (`pads/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single.gds`, this repository; the cell in the chip
GDS is this cell unchanged), made the same way as the S2D pad's PEX view.

## Method (= S2D `make magic-pex EXT_MODE=2` + `make sim-cace CACE_SOURCE=pex`, design repository commit 9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9)

1. `sak-pex.sh -d -m 2 -n sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single_pex` on the taped-out GDS: magic, C-coupled (EXT_MODE=2).
2. `scripts/pex_add_port.py ... vss iovss 1`: magic merges the vss band's p+ tie and the iovss ties into one
   substrate node, so the extracted cell has no vss port; vss is added and tied to iovss by 1 Ohm (the sheet's Rsub),
   as for S2D. Ports reordered to the symbol (`sak-pin-reorder.py` with `<cell>_pex.sym` = symbol with type=primitive).
   Result: [s
```

```
# Slot 7: mode start-up at ss / −40 °C, mode table over PVT, bg_core_pd alone

IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (ngspice, IHP OSDI models). This fills three gaps in the slot-level characterization of the
taped-out slot 7 (commit `158afc5afab0056bd6d216a4db3833e34a364949` of this repository):

1. **The ss / −40 °C mode failures** in the designer's tier-1 mode runs (`docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/modes.txt` of the
   design repository: swap, mon, feed, ext, swap+feed `FAILED` at ss −40 °C, "stall at the bandgap's start-up node").
   Question: simulator artefact or a second stable state of the bandgap (real start-up risk)?
2. **Mode table over more PVT points** than the designer's 3 (modes) / 5 (round trip).
3. **`bg_core_pd` alone**: output current over MOS × rhigh corners × T × V, start-up by supply ramp, supply
   sensitivity, and a check for a zero-current state.

Verdict and numbers: **`results.md`**. Generated tables: `results_tables.md`, CSVs, plots.
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Run CACE parameter groups of sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single on the magic C-coupled PEX netlist, the same way as
# macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff/Makefile target sim-cace CACE_SOURCE=pex (design repository commit
# 9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9): copy of the yaml with own runs / netlist / docs folders and a layout path,
# the prepared netlist (pex_for_sim.py --tie iovss 1e12, subckt renamed to the cell name) newer than the layout, so
# that CACE takes it as up to date and does not extract again.
# Usage: [RSHUNT=<ohm>] cace_pex.sh <macro_dir> <pex_netlist> <layout.gds> <out_dir> <jobs> <group> [<group> ...]
set -u
M=$(realpath "$1"); HERE=$(dirname "$(realpath "$0")"); PEXNET=$(realpath "$2"); GDS=$(realpath "$3"); OUT=$(realpath -m "$4"); JOBS=$5; shift 5
T=sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single
SCR=$M/../../scripts/cace
mkdir -p "$OUT"
cd "$M/verification/cace" || exit 1
xschem -x -q -r --rcfile "$M/schematic/xschem/xschemrc" \
  --command "set fp [open .xschem_library_path_pex w]; puts \$fp \$XSCHEM_LIBRARY_PATH; close \$fp" > /dev/null 2>&1
export XSCHEM_USER_LIBRARY_PATH=$(tr ':' '\n' < .xschem_library_path_pex | grep '/macros/' | grep -v "^$M/" | paste -sd: -)
echo "XSCHEM_USER_LIBRARY_PATH=$XSCHEM_USER_LIBRARY_PATH"
sed -e 's|^  runs:.*|  runs:             cace/_runs_pex|' -e 's|^  documentation:.*|  documentation:    cace/_docs_pex|' \
    -e 's|^  netlist:.*| 
… [3910 more characters cut]
```

```
# Single-ended pad in, differential out: outp - outn = 2 (pad - vref), (outp + outn)/2 = vcm.

# Everything on the ring rails iovdd / iovss; the 1.2 V core ring vdd only feeds the level shifters.
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Fetch the design-repository paths the DC-curve runs need (shallow, blobless; only these paths are downloaded).
# Usage: fetch.sh <work_dir>   -> <work_dir>/design (commit 91a7da8b...: both pad macros, their CACE yaml / templates /
# committed results, schematics of the macros the templates place next to the pads, scripts/cace, the S2D PEX netlist)
# and <work_dir>/tier1 (commit 9052bd35...: docs/slot7_hookup/tier1 and the netlists the slot decks include).
set -euo pipefail
WORK=$(realpath -m "$1")
URL=https://github.com/tatzelbrumm/sg13cmos5l_cm_ip__single2diff2single
C91=91a7da8b67ac56a26c605501146667a1fde28c32
C90=9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9
mkdir -p "$WORK"
if [ ! -d "$WORK/repo" ]; then
  git clone -q --depth 1 --filter=blob:none --no-checkout "$URL" "$WORK/repo"
fi
git -C "$WORK/repo" fetch -q --depth 1 origin $C91
P="scripts/cace scripts/pex_add_port.py"
for t in sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single; do
  P="$P macros/$t/schematic macros/$t/verification/cace macros/$t/netlist"
done
for d in en_ctrl_3v3 bias_sel_pd bias_gen_pd ls_up_1v2_3v3 hv_logic IOPad oa_core_pd bg_core_pd bias_tree_pd \
         bias_tree_noab_pd bias_sel_noab_pd bias_in_pd bias_out_pd bias_oa_pd bias_bg_pd; do
  P="$P macros/$d/schematic/xschem"
done
rm -rf "$WORK/design" && mkdir -p "$WORK/design"
git -C "$WORK/repo" archive $C91 $P | tar x -C "$WORK/design"
git -C "$WORK/repo" fetch -q --depth 1 origin $C90
rm -rf "$WORK/tier1" && mkdir -p "$WORK/tier1"
git -C "$WORK/repo" archive $C90 docs/slot7_hookup/tier1 macros/slot_7/netlist macros/pad_ref_rb/netlist \
  macros/bias_dist_pd/netlist macros/bg_core_pd/netlist macros/en_ctrl_3v3/netlist macros/hv_logic/netlist \
  macros/en_ctrl_3v3/testbenches/spice/run_starve.py macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff/netlist \
  macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/netlist | tar x -C "$WORK/tier1"
echo "fetched: $WORK/design ($C91), $WORK/tier1 ($C90)"
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Write the DC-curve CACE groups next to a pad's own CACE file (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

Usage: mkgroups.py <macro_dir> <cell> <source: schematic|pex>

The sweeps reuse the pad's committed CACE testbench tb_dc (design repository commit 91a7da8b...) unchanged except for
its .control block: same sources (harness iDAC 5 uA into bias_gen_pd, sel 0, en_ctrl_3v3 next to the cell), same
models (MOS corner hv + lv, res / cap / pnp typ, dio_tt), same .options. The new .control block runs one wide DC sweep
and writes it with wrdata to <testbench>_<N>.sweep in the CACE run folder (collect.py reads it); the .data file holds a
dummy 1 (CACE needs a result).

Writes <cace_dir>/_dcg_<cell>[_pex].yaml (copy of the cell yaml: same pins / default_conditions / paths, parameters
replaced by the groups below; pex: own runs / netlist / docs folders and a layout path, as the S2D Makefile's
sim-cace CACE_SOURCE=pex) and <cace_dir>/templates/<cell>_tb_dcsweep.sch.
"""
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

S2D, D2S = 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single'
CORNERS = ['ss', 'sf', 'tt', 'fs', 'ff']
TEMPS = [-40, 27, 125]

# S2D: pad = vref + u through rsrc (50 Ohm); sweep the pad source from -0.3 V to 3.3 V in 2 mV steps.
S2D_CONTROL = r""".control
save v(pad) v(outp) v(outn) v(vcm) v(vref) v(u) i(vio)
dc Vu CACE[-0.3 - CACE\{vref=1.2\}] CACE[3.3 - CACE\{vref=1.2\}] 0.002
set wr_singlescale
set wr_vecnames
option numdgt=9
wrdata CACE\{filename\}_CACE\{N\}.sweep v(u) v(pad) v(outp) v(outn) v(vcm) v(vref) i(vio)
echo 1 > CACE\{filename\}_CACE\{N\}.data
.endc"""

# D2S: vinp - vinn = v(dp) around vicm (vinp = vicm + dp/2, vinn = vicm - dp/2); sweep dp from -3.3 V to 3.3 V in
# 5 mV steps (inputs stay inside 0 ... 3.3 V at vicm = 1.65 V).
D2S_CONTROL = r""".control
save v(pad) v(vinp) v(vinn) v(vref) v(dp) i(vio)
dc Vd CACE[-CACE\{vdmax=3.3\}] CACE\{vdmax=3.3\} 0.005
set wr_singlescale
set wr_vecnames
option numdgt=9
wrdata CACE\{filename\}_CACE\{N\}.sweep v(dp) v(pad) v(vinp) v(vinn) v(vref) i(vio)
echo 1 > CACE\{filename\}_CACE\{N\}.data
.endc"""


def groups(cell):
    g = {}
    if cell == S2D:
        # corner grid at the window_params conditions (vref 0.5 V; vcm, ibias, rsrc, cload at the yaml defaults)
        g['s2d_grid'] = {'vref': {'typical': 0.5}, 'vcm': {'typical': 1.2}, 'vdd': {'enumerate': [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]},
                         'corner_mos': {'enumerate': CORNERS}, 'temp': {'enumerate': TEMPS}}
        # the slot's nominal replica values (pad_ref_rb tt 27 C: vref_s2d about 0.49 V, vcm about 1.26 V)
        g['s2d_slot'] = {'vref': {'typical': 0.49}, 'vcm': {'typical': 1.26}, 'vdd': {'enumerate': [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]},
                         'corner_mos': {'enumerate': CORNERS}, 'temp': {'enumerate': TEMPS}}
        # range vs vref and vcm (the range_params grid: vref 0.5 / 0.8 / 1.2 / 1.65, vcm 1.0 ... 1.65)
        g['s2d_vrefvcm'] = {'vref': {'enumerate': [0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.65]}, 'vcm': {'enumerate': [1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.65]},
                            'vdd': {'enumerate': [3.0, 3.3]}, 'corner_mos': {'enumerate': ['ss', 'tt', 'ff']},
                            'temp': {'enumerate': [-40, 27, 125]}}
    else:
        # corner grid at the dc_params / dc_3v0_params conditions (vref = vicm = vterm 1.65 V, 1 kOhm to vterm,
        # 100 pF), iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 / 3.6 V
        g['d2s_grid'] = {'vdd': {'enumerate': [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]}, 'corner_mos': {'enumerate': CORNERS},
                         'temp': {'enumerate': TEMPS}}
        # unloaded (1 TOhm to vterm instead of 1 kOhm)
        g['d2s_noload'] = {'rload': {'typical': 1e12}, 'vdd': {'enumerate': [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]},
                           'corner_mos': {'enumerate': CORNERS}, 'temp': {'enumerate': TEMPS}}
        # the slot's operating point: vref = vicm = vcm 1.26 V, 1 kOhm to vref (vterm 1.26 V); dp to +-2.52 V keeps
        # the inputs
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Run DC-curve groups (mkgroups.py) of one pad with CACE 2.11 on its schematic or magic PEX netlist, then collect the
# sweeps (collect.py). IHP sg13cmos5l simulation.
# Usage: [JOBS=3] cace_dc.sh <work_dir> <cell> <schematic|pex> <group> [<group> ...]
#   pex netlists: S2D = the design repository's netlist/pex/<cell>_magic_pex_2.spice (commit 91a7da8b, made by the
#   S2D Makefile's magic-pex EXT_MODE=2 from the taped-out GDS); D2S = ../d2s_pex/<cell>_magic_pex_2.spice (this
#   repository, made by d2s_pex/run.sh from pads/<cell>/<cell>.gds). Both prepared as the S2D Makefile's
#   sim-cace CACE_SOURCE=pex: pex_for_sim.py --tie iovss 1e12, subckt renamed to the cell name, the prepared netlist
#   newer than the layout (CACE does not extract again). D2S PEX only: ngspice option rshunt=1e12 (as d2s_pex pass 2;
#   without it the PEX operating point did not converge).
set -u
WORK=$(realpath "$1"); T=$2; SRC=$3; shift 3
HERE=$(dirname "$(realpath "$0")"); SLOT=$(cd "$HERE/../../.." && pwd)
JOBS=${JOBS:-3}
M=$WORK/design/macros/$T
SCR=$WORK/design/scripts/cace
OUT=$WORK/out/$SRC
mkdir -p "$OUT"
cd "$M/verification/cace" || exit 1
python3 "$HERE/mkgroups.py" "$M" "$T" "$SRC" > /dev/null || exit 1
xschem -x -q -r --rcfile "$M/schematic/xschem/xschemrc" \
  --command "set fp [open .xschem_library_path_dcg w]; puts \$fp \$XSCHEM_LIBRARY_PATH; close \$fp" > /dev/null 2>&1
export XSCHEM_USER_LIBRARY_PATH=$(tr ':' '\n' < .xschem_library_path_dcg | grep '/macros/' | grep -v "^$M/" | paste -sd: -)
if [ "$SRC" = pex ]; then
  Y=_dcg_${T}_pex.yaml; RUNS=_runs_dcg_pex
  if [ "$T" = sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff ]; then PEXNET=$M/netlist/pex/${T}_magic_pex_2.spice
  else PEXNET=$SLOT/verification/c2_gaps/d2s_pex/${T}_magic_pex_2.spice; fi
  mkdir -p layout_pex netlist_pex/pex
  ln -sf "$SLOT/pads/$T/$T.gds" layout_pex/$T.gds
  python3 "$SCR/pex_for_sim.py" "$PEXNET" netlist_pex/pex/$T.spice --tie iovss 1e12
  sed -i "s/\b${T}_pex\b/$T/g" netlist_pex/pex/$T.spice
  if [ "$T" = sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single ]; then
    sed -i 's/^\.option warn=1$/.option warn=1\n.option rshunt=1e12/' templates/${T}_tb_dcsweep.sch
  fi
  touch netlist_pex/pex/$T.spice
else
  Y=_dcg_$T.yaml; RUNS=_runs_dcg
fi
status=0
for g in "$@"; do
  rm -rf $RUNS/RUN_*
  echo "=== $T $SRC $g start $(date -u +%H:%M:%S)"
  cace $Y -s $SRC -p "$g" -j "$JOBS" --no-progress-bar > "$OUT/log_$g.txt" 2>&1 || status=1
  python3 "$HERE/collect.py" $RUNS/RUN_* "$g" "$OUT/$g.csv.gz" || status=1
  echo "=== $T $SRC $g done $(date -u +%H:%M:%S)"
done
exit $status
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Collect the wrdata sweeps of one CACE DC-curve group into one gzipped CSV (work folder only, not committed).

Usage: collect.py <RUN_dir> <group> <out.csv.gz>
Each parameters/<group>/run_*/ holds conditions.yaml and <testbench>_<N>.sweep (wrdata, single scale, vector names).
Output columns: run, the group's varying conditions (corner_mos, temp, vdd, vref, vcm, ...), then the sweep columns.
Runs without a sweep (ngspice failed) are listed on stderr and in <out>.failed.txt.
"""
import csv
import glob
import gzip
import os
import re
import sys

import yaml

KEYS = ['corner_mos', 'temp', 'vdd', 'vref', 'vcm', 'vicm', 'vterm', 'rload']


def main(rundir, group, out):
    runs = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, 'parameters', group, 'run_*')),
                  key=lambda p: int(re.search(r'run_(\d+)', p).group(1)))
    failed, n = [], 0
    with gzip.open(out, 'wt', newline='') as f:
        w = None
        for r in runs:
            c = yaml.safe_load(open(os.path.join(r, 'conditions.yaml')))
            sw = glob.glob(os.path.join(r, '*.sweep'))
            if not sw or os.path.getsize(sw[0]) == 0:
                failed.append(f'{os.path.basename(r)} {c}')
                continue
            lines = open(sw[0]).read().split('\n')
            head = lines[0].split()
            if w is None:
                w = csv.writer(f)
                w.writerow(['run'] + KEYS + head)
            cond = [c.get(k, '') for k in KEYS]
            for l in lines[1:]:
                t = l.split()
                if len(t) == len(head):
                    w.writerow([n] + cond + t)
            n += 1
    with open(out + '.failed.txt', 'w') as f:
        f.write(''.join(x + '\n' for x in failed))
    print(f'{group}: {n} of {len(runs)} runs -> {out}' + (f' (failed: {len(failed)})' if failed else ''))
    for x in failed:
        print('FAILED', x, file=sys.stderr)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) != 4:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    main(*sys.argv[1:])
```

## User (or harness) — 2026-11-24 22:09:25 UTC

Base directory for this skill: /tmp/claude-0/bundled-skills/2.1.331/9b4471322a1991e1a16df6fe80e195fa/dataviz

# Data Visualization

A chart is **read by people and executed by you**. This skill turns "make it look
good" into a procedure with checks, so the result is right by construction rather
than by taste.

**The method here is design-system-agnostic.** Nothing in the procedure, the form
heuristic, the six checks, or the mark specs is specific to one product. A design
system supplies a small set of *parameters* (its ramps, a categorical order, a
diverging pair, a status palette, a texture, its surfaces, its filter components);
the method consumes them unchanged. A **validated default palette** is the
reference instance, fully specified in `references/palette.md`. To target your
brand, read that file's structure and substitute its values - touch nothing else.

> The single most important habit: **the color part is computable, so compute it.**
> Never eyeball whether a palette is colorblind-safe - run `scripts/validate_palette.js`.

## The procedure - do these in order

Color comes LAST. Most bad charts pick colors first.

1. **Pick the form.** What is the data's job - magnitude, identity, polarity, a
   single headline, change-over-time? The job picks the chart type, and sometimes
   the answer is *not a chart* (a stat tile or hero number). -> `references/choosing-a-form.md`
2. **Assign color by the job it does.** Categorical (identity), sequential
   (magnitude), diverging (polarity), or status (state) - each has one rule.
   Assign categorical hues in fixed order, never cycled. -> `references/color-formula.md`
3. **VALIDATE the palette - run the script, don't reason about Delta E.**
   `node scripts/validate_palette.js "<hex,hex,...>" --mode light` (relative to
   this skill's base directory - or load it as `<script type="module">` in the
   chart's own page, where it reads
   `data-palette` off `<body>` and logs a `console.table` report). It returns
   pass/fail on the lightness band, chroma floor, adjacent-pair CVD separation,
   the normal-vision floor, and contrast. Fix anything that FAILs before continuing. Re-run for
   `--mode dark` with that mode's surface.
4. **Apply mark specs & spacers.** Thin marks, 4px rounded data-ends anchored to
   the baseline, 2px lines, >=8px markers, a 2px surface gap between fills (stacked
   segments and adjacent bars alike) and a 2px surface ring on overlapping marks,
   selective direct labels. -> `references/marks-and-anatomy.md`
5. **Add the hover layer - by default.** An HTML/SVG chart *is* interactive; ship
   a crosshair+tooltip on line/area and a per-mark hover tooltip on bar/dot/cell.
   The only form that skips it is a bare stat tile with no plot. Hit targets bigger
   than the mark; filters in one row above the charts. -> `references/interaction.md`
6. **Final accessibility pass.** For >= 2 series a legend is always present and <= 4
   are also direct-labeled (a single series needs no legend box - the title names
   it), so identity is never color-alone; a table view exists; dark mode is **selected** - its own
   steps from the same ramps, validated against the dark surface, not an automatic
   flip; texture is available for the CVD/print/forced-colors case.
   One exception to direct name labels: the tall segmented bar (*Figures* in `references/marks-and-anatomy.md`).
7. **Render it and look at it.** The validator checks color, not layout - open or
   screenshot the output and eyeball it for label collisions, geometry, and overflow
   before calling it done.

Then check the result against **`references/anti-patterns.md`** - it is the catalog
of what goes wrong. If your chart matches an entry, it's wrong.

## Non-negotiables (true in every design system)

- **Assign categorical hues in fixed order, never cycled.** A 9th series is never a
  generated hue - it folds into "Other," small multiples, or composite encoding.
- **One axis.** Never a dual-axis chart (two y-scales). Two measures of different
  scale -> two charts, small multiples, or indexed to a common base. *(This is the
  #1 chart mistake - see anti-patterns.)*
- **Color follows the entity, never its rank.** A filter that changes the series
  count must not repaint the survivors.
- **Sequential = one hue, light->dark. Diverging = two hues + a neutral gray
  midpoint.** Never a rainbow; never a hue at the diverging midpoint.
- **Run the validator before shipping any categorical palette.** CVD Delta E >= 8 is the
  target (OKLab ×100); 6-8 is a floor that is legal ONLY with secondary encoding. A
  normal-vision floor below 15 is a hard FAIL - full-color readers can't tell the
  pair apart; re-step it on the adjacent pairlist (secondary encoding does not excuse
  this one); under `--pairs all` cut series or facet instead - see check 4. A contrast WARN
  obligates visible labels or a table view - it is not dismissable.
- **Thin marks; a legend always present for >= 2 series (none for one), with
  selective direct labels (never a number on every point); recessive grid/axes.**
  One exception, legend kept: the tall segmented bar (*Figures* in `references/marks-and-anatomy.md`).
- **Text wears text tokens, never the series color** - values, labels, and legends
  stay in primary/secondary/muted ink; a colored mark beside them carries identity.
- **Status colors are reserved** (good/warning/serious/critical) and never reused
  for "series 4"; they ship with an icon + label, never color alone.

## Plugging in a design system

The method is invariant; only these parameters change per system. The reference
instance - every value filled in - is `references/palette.md`.

| Parameter | What the system provides |
|---|---|
| **Ramps** | the hue scales (named steps) the palette draws from |
| **Categorical theme** | the fixed hue order (a named theme); default + alternates |
| **Sequential hue** | the default single hue for magnitude |
| **Diverging pair** | two warm/cool poles + a neutral midpoint |
| **Status palette** | good / warning / serious / critical - steps distinct from categorical, except that dark-mode good is the series green |
| **Texture fill** | one directional hand-drawn fill, used at 45° / 135° |
| **Surfaces** | light & dark chart-surface colors (the validator needs these) |
| **Filter controls** | date-range & dimension controls (behavioral spec in `interaction.md`) |

To onboard a new system: fill those rows, feed its ramps to the validator, and let
it snap each slot to the nearest passing step. Structure and rules stay as written.

## Reference files

| File | What it answers |
|------|-----------------|
| `references/choosing-a-form.md` | Which chart type / is it even a chart? |
| `references/color-formula.md` | The four jobs, the six checks, snap-to-passing |
| `references/marks-and-anatomy.md` | Mark specs, spacers, labels, figures, hero number |
| `references/interaction.md` | Tooltips & hover, filters & time ranges |
| `references/components.md` | The pieces a chart is made of - build each in plain HTML |
| `references/anti-patterns.md` | **What goes wrong - check every chart against this** |
| `references/palette.md` | **The reference palette instance** - every parameter, filled in; swap for your brand's |
| `scripts/validate_palette.js` | Runnable six-checks validator (run it; don't eyeball) |

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Shared helpers for the DC-curve tables and plots: loading the collected sweeps, range criterion, plot style."""
import csv
import gzip
from collections import OrderedDict

import numpy as np
import matplotlib

matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402

LABEL = {'schematic': 'IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic)', 'pex': 'IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (magic PEX)',
         'klayout': 'IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (KLayout-extracted pads, as the designer\'s tier-1 deck)'}
VIEWNAME = {'schematic': 'schematic', 'pex': 'magic PEX (C-coupled)', 'klayout': 'KLayout-extracted'}
# one fixed color per MOS corner (validated categorical order), line style per temperature
CORNERS = ['tt', 'ss', 'ff', 'sf', 'fs']
COLOR = {'tt': '#2a78d6', 'ss': '#eb6834', 'ff': '#1baf7a', 'sf': '#eda100', 'fs': '#e87ba4'}
LS = {-40: ':', 27: '-', 125: '--'}
VCOLOR = {3.0: '#eb6834', 3.3: '#2a78d6', 3.6: '#1baf7a'}


def style():
    plt.rcParams.update({'figure.dpi': 150, 'savefig.dpi': 150, 'font.size': 9, 'axes.titlesize': 9.5,
                         'axes.grid': True, 'grid.color': '#d8d8d4', 'grid.linewidth': 0.6, 'axes.edgecolor': '#52514e',
                         'axes.labelcolor': '#0b0b0b', 'xtick.color': '#52514e', 'ytick.color': '#52514e',
                         'lines.linewidth': 1.4, 'legend.fontsize': 7.5, 'legend.framealpha': 0.9,
                         'svg.fonttype': 'none', 'figure.facecolor': 'white'})


def load(path):
    """-> OrderedDict key -> dict of numpy arrays; key = (corner, temp, vdd, vref, vcm, vicm, vterm, rload) as typed."""
    runs = OrderedDict()
    with gzip.open(path, 'rt') as f:
        r = csv.reader(f)
        head = next(r)
        keys = head[1:9]
        cols = head[9:]
        rows = {}
        for row in r:
            rows.setdefault(int(row[0]), []).append(row)
    for n, rr in rows.items():
        c = rr[0][1:9]
        key = (c[0], int(float(c[1])), float(c[2]),
               *(float(x) if x not in ('', 'None') else None for x in c[3:8]))
        a = np.array([[float(x) for x in row[9:]] for row in rr])
        runs[key] = {k: a[:, i] for i, k in enumerate(cols)}
    return runs


def ss_line(x, y, x0=0.0, dx=0.1):
    """Small-signal line of y(x) as in the CACE tb_dc: slope from x0 +- dx, value at x0."""
    yp, ym, y0 = np.interp([x0 + dx, x0 - dx, x0], x, y)
    return (yp - ym) / (2 * dx), y0


def contiguous_range(x, err, lim, x0=0.0):
    """Contiguous range of x around x0 where |err| < lim; ends linearly interpolated at |err| = lim.
    Returns (lo, hi, lo_clipped, hi_clipped): *_clipped = True if the criterion holds up to the end of the sweep."""
    a = np.abs(err)
    i0 = int(np.argmin(np.abs(x - x0)))
    if a[i0] >= lim:
        return np.nan, np.nan, False, False
    i = i0
    while i + 1 < len(x) and a[i + 1] < lim:
        i += 1
    if i + 1 < len(x):
        hi, hic = x[i] + (lim - a[i]) * (x[i + 1] - x[i]) / (a[i + 1] - a[i]), False
    else:
        hi, hic = x[i], True
    i = i0
    while i - 1 >= 0 and a[i - 1] < lim:
        i -= 1
    if i - 1 >= 0:
        lo, loc = x[i] + (lim - a[i]) * (x[i - 1] - x[i]) / (a[i - 1] - a[i]), False
    else:
        lo, loc = x[i], True
    return lo, hi, loc, hic


def save(fig, path, svg=True):
    fig.savefig(path + '.png')
    if svg:
        fig.savefig(path + '.svg')
    plt.close(fig)


def md_table(head, rows):
    out = ['| ' + ' | '.join(head) + ' |', '|' + '---|' * len(head)]
    out += ['| ' + ' | '.join(str(x) for x in r) + ' |' for r in rows]
    return '\n'.join(out)
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""S2D pad sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff: DC-transfer plots, range table, summary CSV (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

Usage: s2d.py <work_out_dir> <dest_dir> [<group>]   (group default s2d_grid; reads <work_out_dir>/{schematic,pex}/<group>.csv.gz)

Quantities per run (u = pad source - vref, pad = the pad node behind the 50 Ohm source; vd = outp - outn,
vc = (outp + outn)/2):
  Gain, vd0  small-signal line of vd(u) as the CACE tb_dc: slope from u = +-0.1 V, value at u = 0
  err_ss     (vd - vd0 - Gain u) / Gain: deviation from the small-signal line, referred to the input (CACE INL / window)
  err_id     vd/2 - (pad - vref): deviation from the ideal transfer, referred to the input (includes gain error, offset)
  range      contiguous pad range around pad = vref with |err_ss| < 1 mV (= the CACE window_params criterion; the CACE
             testbench starts its sweep at pad = 0 V, this one at -0.3 V, so Vin_min < 0 is possible here)
"""
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import (COLOR, CORNERS, LABEL, LS, VCOLOR, VIEWNAME, contiguous_range, load, md_table, plt, save,  # noqa
                    ss_line, style)

LIM = 1e-3


def metrics(d, vref):
    u, pad = d['v(u)'], d['v(pad)']
    vd = d['v(outp)'] - d['v(outn)']
    vc = (d['v(outp)'] + d['v(outn)']) / 2 - d['v(vcm)']
    G, vd0 = ss_line(u, vd)
    err_ss = (vd - vd0 - G * u) / G
    err_id = vd / 2 - (pad - vref)
    lo, hi, loc, hic = contiguous_range(u, err_ss, LIM)
    vc0 = float(np.interp(0, u, vc))
    m = (pad >= 0) & (pad <= 1.0)
    inl05 = np.abs(err_ss[np.abs(u) <= 0.5 + 1e-9]).max()
    return dict(u=u, pad=pad, vd=vd, vc=vc, G=G, vd0=vd0, err_ss=err_ss, err_id=err_id, lo=lo + vref, hi=hi + vref,
                loc=loc, hic=hic, vc0=vc0, iio=-float(np.interp(0, u, d['i(vio)'])),
                eid01=np.abs(err_id[m]).max(), inl05=inl05)


def fmt_lo(m):
    return ('<= %.3f' % m['lo']) if m['loc'] else '%.3f' % m['lo']


def transfer_plot(view, k, d, m, dest, cond):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 4.6))
    ax.axvspan(m['lo'], m['hi'], color='#2a78d6', alpha=0.08, lw=0)
    ax.plot(m['pad'], d['v(outp)'], color='#2a78d6', label='outp')
    ax.plot(m['pad'], d['v(outn)'], color='#eb6834', label='outn')
    ax.plot(m['pad'], m['vd'], color='#0b0b0b', label='outp - outn')
    ax.plot(m['pad'], (d['v(outp)'] + d['v(outn)']) / 2, color='#1baf7a', ls='--', label='(outp + outn)/2')
    ax.plot(m['pad'], 2 * (m['pad'] - cond['vref']), color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls=':', label='ideal 2 (pad - vref)')
    for x in (m['lo'], m['hi']):
        ax.axvline(x, color='#2a78d6', lw=0.8)
    ax.axvline(cond['vref'], color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls='-.')
    ax.text((m['lo'] + m['hi']) / 2, 2.85, 'linear range %s ... %.3f V\n(|error from small-signal line| < 1 mV, input-referred)'
            % (fmt_lo(m).replace('<= ', '<='), m['hi']), ha='center', va='top', fontsize=7.5, color='#0b0b0b')
    ax.text(cond['vref'] + 0.03, -1.85, 'vref = %.2f V' % cond['vref'], fontsize=7.5, color='#52514e')
    ax.set_xlim(-0.3, 3.3)
    ax.set_ylim(-2.0, 3.0)
    ax.set_xlabel('pad input voltage [V]')
    ax.set_ylabel('output voltage [V]')
    ax.set_title('S2D pad DC transfer: %s\n%s, %d °C, iovdd %.1f V, vref %.2f V, vcm %.2f V, iref 5 µA'
                 % (LABEL[view], k[0], k[1], k[2], cond['vref'], cond['vcm']))
    ax.legend(loc='lower right', ncol=1)
    fig.tight_layout()
    save(fig, str(dest / 'plots' / f's2d_transfer_tt27_{view}'))


def corner_plot(view, runs, ms, dest, cond, vdd=3.3):
    fig, (a1, a2) = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(7.2, 7.0), sharex=True, gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [1, 1.25]})
    for k, m in ms.items():
        if k[2] != vdd:
            continue
        kw = dict(color=COLOR[k[0]], ls=LS[k[1]], lw=1.1)
        a1.plot(m['pad'], m['vd'], **kw)
       
… [7728 more characters cut]
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Cross-check the DC-curve summaries against the committed CACE results of the design repository (commit 91a7da8b).

Usage: crosscheck.py <design_tree> <dest_dir>  -> <dest_dir>/tables/crosscheck.md
S2D: Vin_min / Vin_max of window_params (schematic and _pex; vref 0.5 V; CACE sweeps the pad from 0 V in 10 mV steps,
     so its Vin_min is 0 where the criterion holds at 0 V) against the same criterion on the 2 mV sweeps here
     (Vin_min here clipped to >= 0 for the comparison).
D2S: Gain and INL (deviation from the small-signal line over vd = +-1 V, gain from +-0.2 V) of dc_params (3.3 / 3.6 V)
     and dc_3v0_params (3.0 V), schematic only (no committed PEX CSV in the design repository; ../d2s_pex/csv has the
     PEX dc_params of this repository), against the same quantities on the 5 mV sweeps here.
"""
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def rd(p):
    with open(p) as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(l for l in f if not l.startswith('#')))


def main(tree, dest):
    tree, dest = Path(tree), Path(dest)
    out = []
    res = tree / 'macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff/verification/cace/results'
    s = dest / 'csv' / 's2d_grid_summary.csv'
    if s.exists():
        mine = {(r['view'], r['corner_mos'], int(float(r['temp'])), float(r['vdd'])): r for r in rd(s)}
        out.append('### S2D window_params (committed CACE) vs this sweep\n')
        out.append('| view | runs compared | max abs diff Vin_max [mV] | max abs diff Vin_min (>= 0) [mV] | '
                   'committed Vin_max min ... max [V] | here min ... max [V] |')
        out.append('|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
        for view, sub in (('schematic', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff'), ('pex', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff_pex')):
            n, dmax, dmin, cv, hv = 0, 0, 0, [], []
            for r in rd(res / sub / 'window_params.csv'):
                k = (view, r['corner_mos'], int(float(r['temp'])), float(r['vdd']))
                if k not in mine:
                    continue
                m = mine[k]
                n += 1
                dmax = max(dmax, abs(float(m['Vin_max_V']) - float(r['Vin_max[V]'])))
                dmin = max(dmin, abs(max(float(m['Vin_min_V']), 0) - float(r['Vin_min[V]'])))
                cv.append(float(r['Vin_max[V]'])); hv.append(float(m['Vin_max_V']))
            if n:
                out.append(f'| {view} | {n} | {dmax*1e3:.2f} | {dmin*1e3:.2f} | {min(cv):.4f} ... {max(cv):.4f} | '
                           f'{min(hv):.4f} ... {max(hv):.4f} |')
    resd = tree / 'macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/verification/cace/results/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single'
    s = dest / 'csv' / 'd2s_grid_summary.csv'
    if s.exists():
        mine = {(r['view'], r['corner_mos'], int(float(r['temp'])), float(r['vdd'])): r for r in rd(s)}
        out.append('\n### D2S dc_params / dc_3v0_params (committed CACE, schematic) vs this sweep\n')
        out.append('| view here | runs compared | max abs diff Gain | max abs diff INL [mV] | committed INL min ... max [mV] '
                   '| here INL min ... max [mV] |')
        out.append('|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
        for view in ('schematic', 'pex'):
            n, dg, di, ci, hi = 0, 0, 0, [], []
            for f in ('dc_params.csv', 'dc_3v0_params.csv'):
                for r in rd(resd / f):
                    k = (view, r['corner_mos'], int(float(r['temp'])), float(r['vdd']))
                    if k not in mine:
                        continue
                    m = mine[k]
                    n += 1
                    dg = max(dg, abs(float(m['Gain']) - float(r['Gain[]'])))
                    # the committed CSV column says mV but holds V (cace_to_csv.py writes the testbench's SI value)
                    di = max(di, abs(float(m['INL_pm1V_mV']) - float(r['INL[mV]']) * 1e3))
                    ci.append(float(r['INL[mV]']) * 1e3); hi.append(float(m['INL_pm1V_m
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Round trip pad in -> S2D -> slot 7 (default mode) -> D2S -> pad out, DC sweep (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

Usage: python3 -I roundtrip.py <work_dir> <out_dir> <job> [<job> ...]
  job = <view>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>     view = klayout | schematic | pex (the two custom pads only, see below)
  e.g. schematic:tt:27:3.3. Writes <out_dir>/rt_<view>_<mos>_<temp>_<vdd>.csv (sweep) and .log (ngspice output).

The deck is the designer's tier-1 slot deck (design repository commit 9052bd35, docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_tier1.py;
imported from there, so the slot glue and the other blocks are the same): slot_7 (source netlist) in its default mode
(all mode bits 0, enable 1.2 V), pad_ref_rb and bias_dist_pd from their KLayout-extracted netlists (converted and wrapped
by run_tier1.wrapped()), bg_core_pd schematic, ls_up_fs / en_ctrl_3v3 / hv_logic source netlists; vdd_3v3 = iovdd;
S2D vref / vcm and D2S vref come from the slot's replica bias pad_ref_rb (not set by sources), iref from bg_core_pd
through bias_dist_pd. Pad out unloaded (1 Gohm to ground, as run_tier1.py). Models: MOS corner on hv and lv, res_typ,
pnp typ, diodes.lib (as run_tier1.py), plus the IHP IO library sg13cmos5l_io.spice and cap_typ (needed by the
schematic pad netlists; as the pads' CACE templates).

Pad views (both pads always the same view):
  klayout    netlist/layout/<cell>_klayout.cir at 9052bd35 (the designer's tier-1 view, converted by run_tier1.wrapped())
  schematic  the CACE schematic netlists of the pads at 91a7da8b (cace/netlist/schematic/<cell>.spice, written by
             cace_dc.sh's schematic runs)
  pex        the magic C-coupled PEX netlists as prepared by cace_dc.sh's pex runs (cace/netlist_pex/pex/<cell>.spice:
             S2D from the design repository, D2S from ../d2s_pex), with ngspice option rshunt=1e12 (needed by the D2S
             PEX netlist, see ../d2s_pex/README.md)
The pads' internal subcircuits (unit_r2 appears in both pads) get a per-pad prefix, so that the two netlists do not
redefine each other's subcircuits.

Sweep: pad in (Vin) -0.3 ... 3.3 V in 5 mV steps. Error = pad_out - (v(vcm_s2d) + v(padin) - v(vref_s2d)), the
designer's expected value with the replica's actual vcm / vref (run_tier1.py), and err_ss = deviation from the
small-signal line (slope from pad = vref_s2d +- 0.1 V).
"""
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WORK, OUT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve(), Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
JOBS = sys.argv[3:]
T1 = WORK / 'tier1' / 'docs' / 'slot7_hookup' / 'tier1'
sys.path.insert(0, str(T1))
import run_tier1 as T  # noqa: E402

PADS = ['sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single']
RUNS = WORK / 'rt_runs'
PDK = '/foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l'


def prefixed(src, dst, cell, pfx):
    """Copy a pad netlist with every subckt except the pad cell renamed to <pfx>_<name> (definitions and calls)."""
    txt = Path(src).read_text()
    names = [n for n in re.findall(r'^\.subckt\s+(\S+)', txt, re.M | re.I) if n != cell]
    for n in names:
        txt = re.sub(r'(?<![\w.#$])' + re.escape(n) + r'(?![\w.#$])', f'{pfx}_{n}', txt)
    Path(dst).write_text(txt)
    return dst


def includes(view):
    RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    T.RUNS = T.RUNS.__class__(RUNS)
    other = [T.wrapped(c) for c in ('pad_ref_rb', 'bias_dist_pd')]
    if view == 'klayout':
        pads = [T.wrapped(c) for c in PADS]
    else:
        pads = []
        for c, pfx in zip(PADS, ('s2dpad', 'd2spad')):
            d = 'netlist/schematic' if view == 'schematic' else 'netlist_pex/pex'
            src = WORK / 'design' / 'macros' / c / 'verification' / 'cace' / d / f'{c}.spice'
            pads.append(prefixed(src, RUNS / f'{c}_{view}.spice', c, pfx))
    return [str(RUNS / p) for p in other] + [str(p) for p in pads]


def run(job):
    view, c, t, v = job.split(':')
    t, v = int(t), float(v)
    tag = f'rt_{view}
```

```
<!--

SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0

# DC curves of the two custom pads: input / output range and linearity

**IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic | magic PEX)**, ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models, run through CACE 2.11 on
the pads' own committed CACE testbenches. Pads: `sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff` (S2D, `s7_an[0]`) and
`sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single` (D2S, `s7_an[1]`) as taped out (`pads/<cell>/<cell>.gds` of this repository; the GDS
files are byte-identical to the design repository's `layout/klayout/<cell>.klay.gds` at commit 91a7da8b, git blob
`c871a046…` / `d09ed053…`). Numbers and range tables: **[results.md](results.md)**.

Status: S2D figures done; D2S figures **running**; round trip **running**; range vs vref / vcm **running**. Figures
marked "running" below do not exist yet.

## Figures (plots/, PNG 150 dpi, SVG where it stays below 150 kB)

| file | status | what it shows |
|---|---|---|
| `s2d_transfer_tt27_schematic`, `s2d_transfer_tt27_pex` | done | S2D: outp, outn, outp − outn, (outp + outn)/2 and the ideal 2 (pad − vref) vs the pad voltage −0.3 … 3.3 V; tt, 27 °C, iovdd 3.3 V, vref 0.5 V, vcm 1.2 V; the linear input range (1 mV criterion) shaded |
| `s2d_corners_schematic`, `s2d_corners_pex` | done | S2D: top, outp − outn vs pad 0 … 1.3 V; bottom, error vs the ideal transfer, vd/2 − (pad − vref), input-referred, symlog mV axis (linear inside ±1 mV, shaded); 5 MOS corners (color) × −40 / 27 / 125 °C (line style), iovdd 3.3 V |
| `s2d_linearity_criterion` | done | S2D: \|error from the small-signal line\| (the range criterion), input-referred, log axis, schematic and PEX side by side; where a curve crosses 1 mV is the range end; iovdd 3.3 V |
| `s2d_range` | done | S2D: linear input range (1 mV criterion) as a bar per MOS corner × temperature × iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 / 3.6 V, schematic and PEX |
| `d2s_*` | running | D2S transfer, error vs ideal, output range |
| `rt_*` | running | round trip S2D → slot → D2S |
| `s2d_vrefvcm_*` | running | S2D range vs vref and vcm |

How to read the error plots: "error vs ideal" includes the systematic gain error (S2D gain 1.9968 … 1.9978 instead of
2, i.e. −0.11 … −0.16 %) and offset, so it is a straight sloped line inside the linear range (0 at pad = vref) and bends
away at the range ends; "error from the small-signal line" removes gain and offset (the CACE definition of INL) and
shows only the nonlinearity, which is what limits the range.

## Range criteria

- **S2D input range** = the contiguous pad-voltage range around pad = vref in which |vd − vd0 − G·u| / G < 1 mV,
  with u = pad source − vref, vd = outp − outn, G = (vd(u = +0.1 V) − vd(u = −0.1 V)) / 0.2 V and vd0 = vd(u = 0).
  This is exactly the CACE `window_params` criterion of the S2D yaml (template `tb_range`) and the CACE `INL`
  definition of `tb_dc`, so the table reproduces the committed `window_params` results (cross-check in results.md:
  within 0.62 mV on Vin_max, the difference being the 10 mV vs 2 mV sweep steps). Differences: the sweep here starts at
  −0.3 V (CACE: 0 V), so Vin_min < 0 V is reported where the criterion still holds below ground; and it runs at
  iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 / 3.6 V over all five MOS corners (CACE window: ss / tt / ff).
- D2S output range: see the D2S section (running).

## Conditions (= the committed CACE testbench `tb_dc` of each pad, only the analysis changed)

`mkgroups.py` copies the pad's `verification/cace/templates/<cell>_tb_dc.sch` (design commit 91a7da8b) to
`<cell>_tb_dcsweep.sch` and replaces only its `.control` block (one wide `dc` sweep, `wrdata`); sources, models and
options are unchanged:

- MOS corner on `cornerMOShv` and `cornerMOSlv`, `res_typ`, `cap_typ`, pnp `typ`, `dio_tt`, IHP IO library
  `sg13cmos5l_io.spice`; `.options method=gear reltol=1e-4 abstol=1e-15 gmin=1e-15` (+ `klu` in the D2S template).
- Bias: harness iDAC (PMOS mirror) 5 µA into `bias_gen_pd` with sel = 0 (harness current passed to `iref`),
  `en_ctrl
```

```
<!--
SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Results: DC curves of the S2D and D2S pads

IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic | magic PEX), ngspice-47, CACE 2.11, 2026-11-24. Method, criteria, conditions and
limits: [README.md](README.md). Every number here is from the runs of `run.sh` in this folder (tables generated into
`tables/`, per-run values in `csv/`).

Status: S2D done. D2S, round trip, range vs vref / vcm: **running** (sections below marked so).

## 1. S2D pad (sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff): input range and linearity

Conditions: CACE `tb_dc` sources, vref 0.5 V, vcm 1.2 V, harness iref 5 µA, 50 Ω source, 0.5 pF per output; pad
swept −0.3 … 3.3 V in 2 mV steps; 5 MOS corners × −40 / 27 / 125 °C × iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 / 3.6 V = 45 runs per view,
45 / 45 completed in both views.

**Linear input range (|error from the small-signal line| < 1 mV, input-referred; the CACE `window_params`
criterion).** High end 1.057 V (ss −40 °C 3.0 V) … 1.299 V (ff −40 °C 3.6 V) over all 45 runs; at iovdd ≥ 3.3 V
1.154 … 1.299 V (lowest at ss 125 °C). Low end: below 0 V (down to −0.252 V) at −40 and 27 °C; above 0 V at 125 °C,
at most 0.087 V (ff 125 °C 3.6 V). The range at tt 27 °C 3.3 V is −0.149 … 1.245 V. So the documented range
"0 … about 1 V at vref = 0.5 V" holds in every run for the upper end (≥ 1.057 V) and is met at 0 V except at 125 °C,
where the error from the small-signal line reaches 1 mV between pad 0.013 V and 0.087 V depending on the corner.

- Gain (small-signal, from u = ±0.1 V) 1.99676 … 1.99783 (ideal 2; −0.16 … −0.11 %), systematic offset < 1 µV.
- Error vs the ideal 2 (pad − vref), referred to the input, over pad 0 … 1 V: at most 4.34 mV (ff 125 °C, at pad
  = 0 V; at 27 °C and below about 1 mV, mostly the −0.12 % gain error: ≈ 0.6 mV at pad = vref ± 0.5 V).
- Schematic vs magic PEX: identical within 0.03 mV in the range ends and 0.029 mV in the error curves (C-coupled
  extraction, DC).
- Above the range the outputs saturate: at tt 27 °C 3.3 V, outp − outn rises to about 2.7 V with a step near pad
  1.52 V (`s2d_transfer_tt27_*`), the common mode leaves vcm there.

Cross-check with the committed CACE `window_params` (design 91a7da8b, ss / tt / ff × 3 T × 3 V, 27 runs per view):
Vin_max agrees within 0.62 mV (schematic and PEX; CACE sweeps in 10 mV steps, here 2 mV), Vin_min (clipped at 0 V
as CACE) within 0.25 mV; committed Vin_max 1.0575 … 1.2993 V, here 1.0571 … 1.2994 V (`tables/crosscheck.md`).

Range table per corner (schematic; PEX in `tables/s2d_grid.md`, equal to ±0.03 mV):

| corner | T [°C] | 3.0 V | 3.3 V | 3.6 V |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| tt | -40 | -0.090 ... 1.126 | -0.233 ... 1.289 | -0.240 ... 1.291 |
| tt | 27 | -0.098 ... 1.172 | -0.149 ... 1.245 | -0.151 ... 1.245 |
| tt | 125 | 0.050 ... 1.161 | 0.049 ... 1.164 | 0.050 ... 1.163 |
| ss | -40 | -0.032 ... 1.057 | -0.236 ... 1.278 | -0.246 ... 1.283 |
| ss | 27 | -0.059 ... 1.101 | -0.171 ... 1.236 | -0.174 ... 1.236 |
| ss | 125 | 0.018 ... 1.142 | 0.013 ... 1.154 | 0.014 ... 1.153 |

| sf | -40 | -0.114 ... 1.160 | -0.220 ... 1.287 | -0.226 ... 1.288 |
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# DC curves of the S2D / D2S pads (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, schematic | magic PEX): reproduces plots/, csv/, tables/
# of this folder from a checkout of this repository plus the design-repository commits (fetched by fetch.sh).
# Usage: run.sh [<work_dir>] [fetch|s2d|d2s|rt|vrefvcm|plots|all]   (default ./_work, all)
# Environment: JOBS = parallel ngspice runs (default 3). Needs PDK_ROOT with ihp-sg13cmos5l, ngspice with the IHP OSDI
# models, xschem, CACE 2.11, python3 with numpy / matplotlib / pyyaml, git with network access to github.com.
set -euo pipefail
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "${1:-$HERE/_work}")
STEP=${2:-all}
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l
export JOBS=${JOBS:-3}
S2D=sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff
D2S=sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single
mkdir -p "$WORK" "$HERE/plots" "$HERE/csv" "$HERE/tables"

fetch() { "$HERE/fetch.sh" "$WORK"; }

s2d() {
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $S2D schematic s2d_grid
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $S2D pex s2d_grid
  python3 "$HERE/s2d.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE" s2d_grid
}

d2s() {
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $D2S schematic d2s_grid d2s_noload
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $D2S pex d2s_grid d2s_noload
  python3 "$HERE/d2s.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE"
}

rt() {
  # needs the pad netlists that the s2d / d2s steps prepared (schematic and PEX views)
  grep -v '^#' "$HERE/jobs_rt.txt" | xargs -P "$JOBS" -n 1 python3 -I "$HERE/roundtrip.py" "$WORK" "$WORK/out/rt"
  python3 "$HERE/rt.py" "$WORK/out/rt" "$HERE"
}

vrefvcm() {
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $S2D schematic s2d_vrefvcm s2d_slot
  "$HERE/cace_dc.sh" "$WORK" $D2S schematic d2s_slot
  python3 "$HERE/vrefvcm.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE"
}

plots() {
  python3 "$HERE/s2d.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE" s2d_grid
  python3 "$HERE/d2s.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE"
  python3 "$HERE/rt.py" "$WORK/out/rt" "$HERE"
  python3 "$HERE/vrefvcm.py" "$WORK/out" "$HERE"
  python3 "$HERE/crosscheck.py" "$WORK/design" "$HERE"
}

case "$STEP" in
  all) fetch; s2d; d2s; rt; vrefvcm; plots ;;
  fetch|s2d|d2s|rt|vrefvcm|plots) $STEP ;;
  *) echo "unknown step $STEP"; exit 1 ;;
esac
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""D2S pad sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single: DC-transfer plots, output-range table, summary CSV (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

Usage: d2s.py <work_out_dir> <dest_dir>   (reads <work_out_dir>/{schematic,pex}/{d2s_grid,d2s_noload}.csv.gz)

Quantities per run (vd = vinp - vinn, the swept dp; vo = pad - vref; ideal vo = vd/2):
  Gain, vo0  small-signal line of vo(vd) as the CACE tb_dc: slope from vd = +-0.2 V, value at vd = 0
  INL        max |vo - vo0 - Gain vd| over |vd| <= 1 V, output-referred (= CACE dc_params INL, limit 10 mV)
  err_ss     vo - vo0 - Gain vd, output-referred (deviation from the small-signal line)
  err_id     vo - vd/2, output-referred (deviation from the ideal transfer; includes gain error and offset)
  range      contiguous vd range around 0 with |err_ss| < 10 mV (the CACE INL limit), given as the pad-voltage span
             vref + vo at its ends; also with 1 mV
  Vout_max / Vout_min  pad at vd = +3 / -3 V (as CACE drive_params) and at the sweep ends +-3.3 V
"""
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import (COLOR, CORNERS, LABEL, LS, VCOLOR, contiguous_range, load, md_table, plt, save,  # noqa: E402
                    ss_line, style)
from s2d import range_plot  # noqa: E402

LIM, LIM2 = 10e-3, 1e-3


def metrics(d):
    x, pad, vref = d['v(dp)'], d['v(pad)'], d['v(vref)']
    vo = pad - vref
    G, vo0 = ss_line(x, vo, dx=0.2)
    err_ss = vo - vo0 - G * x
    err_id = vo - x / 2
    m = {'x': x, 'pad': pad, 'vo': vo, 'G': G, 'vo0': vo0, 'err_ss': err_ss, 'err_id': err_id, 'vref': vref[0],
         'inl': np.abs(err_ss[np.abs(x) <= 1 + 1e-9]).max(),
         'p3': float(np.interp(3, x, pad)), 'm3': float(np.interp(-3, x, pad)), 'pmax': pad.max(), 'pmin': pad.min(),
         'iio': -float(np.interp(0, x, d['i(vio)']))}
    for tag, lim in (('', LIM), ('1', LIM2)):
        lo, hi, loc, hic = contiguous_range(x, err_ss, lim)
        m['xlo' + tag], m['xhi' + tag] = lo, hi
        m['lo' + tag] = float(np.interp(lo, x, pad))
        m['hi' + tag] = float(np.interp(hi, x, pad))
        m['loc' + tag], m['hic' + tag] = loc, hic
    return m


def transfer_plot(view, k, d_l, m_l, d_u, m_u, dest):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 4.6))
    ax.axvspan(m_l['xlo'], m_l['xhi'], color='#2a78d6', alpha=0.08, lw=0)
    ax.plot(m_l['x'], m_l['pad'], color='#2a78d6', label='pad, 1 kΩ to 1.65 V (CACE load) + 100 pF')
    if m_u is not None:
        ax.plot(m_u['x'], m_u['pad'], color='#eb6834', ls='--', label='pad, unloaded (1 TΩ)')
    ax.plot(m_l['x'], m_l['vref'] + m_l['x'] / 2, color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls=':', label='ideal vref + (vinp - vinn)/2')
    ax.axhline(k[2], color='#52514e', lw=0.6, ls='-.')
    ax.axhline(0, color='#52514e', lw=0.6, ls='-.')
    ax.text(2.2, 0.28, 'output range (1 kΩ):\n%.3f ... %.3f V\n(|error from small-signal line|\n< 10 mV, output-referred)'
            % (m_l['lo'], m_l['hi']), ha='center', fontsize=7.5,
            bbox=dict(boxstyle='round', fc='white', ec='#2a78d6', lw=0.8))
    ax.text(-3.2, k[2] - 0.17, 'iovdd %.1f V' % k[2], fontsize=7.5, color='#52514e')
    ax.text(-3.2, 2.55, 'pad at vinp - vinn = ±3 V:\n1 kΩ: %.3f / %.3f V' % (m_l['m3'], m_l['p3'])
            + ('\nunloaded: %.3f / %.3f V' % (m_u['m3'], m_u['p3']) if m_u is not None else ''), fontsize=7.5,
            va='top')
    ax.set_xlim(-3.3, 3.3)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.1, 3.7)
    ax.set_xlabel('differential input vinp - vinn [V] (around vicm = %.2f V)' % m_l['vref'])
    ax.set_ylabel('pad output voltage [V]')
    ax.set_title('D2S pad DC transfer: %s\n%s, %d °C, iovdd %.1f V, vref = vicm = %.2f V, iref 5 µA'
                 % (LABEL[view], k[0], k[1], k[2], m_l['vref']))
    ax.legend(loc='lower right')
    fig.tight_layout()
    save(fig, str(dest / 'plots' / f'd2s_transfer_tt27_{view}'))
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Round trip S2D -> slot 7 (default mode) -> D2S: plots, range table, summary CSV (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

Usage: rt.py <rt_out_dir> <dest_dir>   (reads rt_<view>_<mos>_<temp>_<vdd>.csv written by roundtrip.py)

expected = v(vcm_s2d) + v(padin) - v(vref_s2d) (replica values as simulated; the designer's run_tier1.py definition)
err      = v(padout) - expected
err_ss   = deviation of padout(padin) from its small-signal line (slope from padin = vref_s2d +- 0.1 V, value at
           vref_s2d); the round-trip gain is 1, so input- and output-referred are the same
range    = contiguous padin range around vref_s2d with |err_ss| < 1 mV (and < 10 mV), with the padout span
"""
import csv
import glob
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import LABEL, VIEWNAME, contiguous_range, md_table, plt, save, ss_line, style  # noqa: E402

PTS = [('tt', 27, 3.3), ('ss', -40, 3.0), ('ff', 125, 3.6), ('fs', 125, 3.6), ('sf', -40, 3.0)]
PCOL = {('tt', 27, 3.3): '#2a78d6', ('ss', -40, 3.0): '#eb6834', ('ff', 125, 3.6): '#1baf7a',
        ('fs', 125, 3.6): '#e87ba4', ('sf', -40, 3.0): '#eda100'}
VLS = {'schematic': '-', 'pex': '--', 'klayout': ':'}
VIEWS = ['schematic', 'pex', 'klayout']
RTLABEL = 'IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, slot 7 tier-1 deck (pads: schematic | magic PEX | KLayout-extracted)'


def load(d):
    runs = {}
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(str(Path(d) / 'rt_*.csv'))):
        m = re.match(r'rt_(\w+?)_(\w\w)_(-?\d+)_([\d.]+)\.csv', Path(f).name)
        lines = open(f).read().split('\n')
        head = lines[0].split()
        a = np.array([[float(x) for x in l.split()] for l in lines[1:] if len(l.split()) == len(head)])
        if len(a) == 0:
            continue
        runs[(m.group(1), m.group(2), int(m.group(3)), float(m.group(4)))] = {h: a[:, i] for i, h in enumerate(head)}
    return runs


def metrics(d):
    x, y = d['v(padin)'], d['v(padout)']
    vref = float(np.interp(0.5, x, d['v(vref_s2d)']))
    vcm = float(np.interp(0.5, x, d['v(vcm_s2d)']))
    vrefd = float(np.interp(0.5, x, d['v(vref_d2s)']))
    exp = d['v(vcm_s2d)'] + x - d['v(vref_s2d)']
    err = y - exp
    G, y0 = ss_line(x, y, x0=vref)
    err_ss = y - y0 - G * (x - vref)
    m = dict(x=x, y=y, err=err, err_ss=err_ss, G=G, vref=vref, vcm=vcm, vrefd=vrefd, off=y0 - vcm,
             emax01=np.abs(err[(x >= 0) & (x <= 1.0 + 1e-9)]).max())
    for tag, lim in (('1', 1e-3), ('10', 10e-3)):
        lo, hi, loc, hic = contiguous_range(x, err_ss, lim, x0=vref)
        m['lo' + tag], m['hi' + tag], m['loc' + tag] = lo, hi, loc
        m['olo' + tag], m['ohi' + tag] = float(np.interp(lo, x, y)), float(np.interp(hi, x, y))
    return m


def main(rdir, dest):
    dest = Path(dest)
    style()
    runs = load(rdir)
    if not runs:
        sys.exit('no round-trip data')
    ms = {k: metrics(d) for k, d in runs.items()}
    # 1. transfer at tt 27 C 3.3 V, all views
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 4.6))
    ref = None
    for v in VIEWS:
        k = (v, 'tt', 27, 3.3)
        if k not in ms:
            continue
        m = ms[k]
        ax.plot(m['x'], m['y'], color={'schematic': '#2a78d6', 'pex': '#eb6834', 'klayout': '#1baf7a'}[v],
                ls=VLS[v], label='pad out, pads %s' % VIEWNAME[v])
        ref = ref or m
    if ref:
        ax.plot(ref['x'], ref['vcm'] + ref['x'] - ref['vref'], color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls='-.',
                label='expected vcm + (pad in - vref)')
        ax.axvspan(ref['lo1'], ref['hi1'], color='#2a78d6', alpha=0.08, lw=0)
        ax.text(2.3, 0.45, 'shaded: |error from small-signal line| < 1 mV\npad in %.3f ... %.3f V -> pad out %.3f ... '
                '%.3f V\n(pads schematic; replica vref %.3f V, vcm %.3f V)' % (ref['lo1'], ref['hi1'], ref['olo1'],
                                                                      
… [4098 more characters cut]
```

```bash
                   'line| < 1 mV, input-referred)\\nvref %.2f V (dash-dot), vcm %.2f V, iref 5 µA; "<" = criterion '
                   'still met at the sweep start -0.3 V' % (cond['vref'], cond['vcm']))
    if group == 's2d_grid':
        criterion_plot(allms, dest, cond)""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""S2D input range vs vref and vcm (the range_params grid), IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic).

Usage: vrefvcm.py <work_out_dir> <dest_dir>   (reads <work_out_dir>/schematic/s2d_vrefvcm.csv.gz)

Grid: vref 0.5 / 0.8 / 1.2 / 1.65 V x vcm 1.0 / 1.2 / 1.4 / 1.65 V x iovdd 3.0 / 3.3 V x MOS ss / tt / ff x
-40 / 27 / 125 C (range_params: vref, vcm, ss / tt / ff, -40 / 125 C at 3.0 V; extended here by 27 C and 3.3 V).
Range per run: as s2d.py (|error from the small-signal line| < 1 mV, input-referred, contiguous around pad = vref;
pad swept -0.3 ... 3.3 V). Plotted per (vref, vcm, iovdd): the guaranteed range over the 9 corner / temperature runs
(highest low end ... lowest high end) as a bar, and tt 27 C as a marker pair.
"""
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import LABEL, load, md_table, plt, save, style  # noqa: E402
from s2d import metrics  # noqa: E402

VREFS = [0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.65]
VCMS = [1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.65]
VCOL = {1.0: '#2a78d6', 1.2: '#eb6834', 1.4: '#1baf7a', 1.65: '#6250d6'}


def main(wout, dest):
    wout, dest = Path(wout), Path(dest)
    style()
    p = wout / 'schematic' / 's2d_vrefvcm.csv.gz'
    runs = load(p)
    ms = {k: metrics(d, k[3]) for k, d in runs.items()}
    vdds = sorted({k[2] for k in ms})
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, len(vdds), figsize=(7.6, 4.8), sharey=True, squeeze=False)
    rows, tab = [], []
    for ax, vdd in zip(axs[0], vdds):
        for i, vref in enumerate(VREFS):
            for j, vcm in enumerate(VCMS):
                sub = {k: m for k, m in ms.items() if k[2] == vdd and k[3] == vref and k[4] == vcm}
                if not sub:
                    continue
                lo = max(m['lo'] for m in sub.values())
                hi = min(m['hi'] for m in sub.values())
                t = next((m for k, m in sub.items() if k[:2] == ('tt', 27)), None)
                x = i + (j - 1.5) * 0.17
                if hi > lo:
                    ax.plot([x, x], [lo, hi], color=VCOL[vcm], lw=3, solid_capstyle='butt')
                else:
                    ax.plot([x], [vref], marker='x', color=VCOL[vcm], ms=6)
                if t is not None:
                    ax.plot([x, x], [t['lo'], t['hi']], ls='none', marker='_', color='#0b0b0b', ms=7, mew=1.2)
                rows.append([vdd, vref, vcm, len(sub), '%.4f' % lo, '%.4f' % hi,
                             '%.4f' % t['lo'] if t else '', '%.4f' % t['hi'] if t else ''])
            ax.plot([i - 0.4, i + 0.4], [vref, vref], color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls='-.')
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(VREFS)))
        ax.set_xticklabels(['%.2f' % v for v in VREFS])
        ax.set_xlabel('vref [V]')
        ax.set_title('iovdd %.1f V' % vdd, fontsize=8.5)
        ax.grid(axis='x', visible=False)
        ax.set_ylim(-0.35, 3.0)
    axs[0][0].set_ylabel('pad input range [V] (1 mV criterion)')
    h = [plt.Line2D([], [], color=VCOL[v], lw=3, label='vcm %.2f V' % v) for v in VCMS]
    h += [plt.Line2D([], [], ls='none', marker='_', color='#0b0b0b', ms=7, mew=1.2, label='tt 27 °C ends'),
          plt.Line2D([], [], ls='none', marker='x', color='#52514e', label='no common range'),
          plt.Line2D([], [], color='#8a8984', lw=0.8, ls='-.', label='pad = vref')]
    fig.legend(handles=h, loc='lower center', ncol=4)
    fig.suptitle('S2D pad: linear input range vs vref and vcm, %s\nbar = range met at all of ss / tt / ff × −40 / 27 / '
                 '125 °C (|error from small-signal line| < 1 mV, input-referred); iref 5 µA'
                 % LABEL['schematic'], fontsize=9.5)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.08, 1, 1))
    save(fig, str(dest / 'plots' / 's2d_vrefvcm_range'))
    head = ['vdd', 'vref', 'vcm', 'runs', 'worst_lo_V', 'worst_hi_V', 'tt27_lo_V', 'tt27_hi_V']
    with open(dest / 'csv' / 's2d_vrefvcm_summary.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:
```
